Oh, goodness, no. I wouldn't say that. I mean... I like to chat with my friends at the church, maybe ask what they're bringing to a potluck just to be polite and coordinate, but... but I certainly don't need someone to hold my hand for everyday things.

Freezing episodes? Oh, I... well, I wouldn't really say I have "episodes," Doctor. That makes it sound so serious, like I'm turning into a statue or something! 

I mean, maybe once in a great while I might just pause for a second, you know, if I'm trying to remember what I walked into a room for. But I certainly don't just freeze up all the time. Goodness, if I did that, I'd never get the church hall swept before Sunday service! 

I really don't think it's anything to worry about. I'm keeping up with everything just fine. I wouldn't want you to write down that I'm having terrible freezing spells when I'm really doing quite well. That makes sense, doesn't it? I hope I'm answering these questions the way you need me to.

Repeating what other people say? Oh, my. No, I... I don't think I do anything like that.

Do I ever find myself doing the same movements over and over? Like tapping or... or fidgeting? 

Oh, goodness, no. I mean, I suppose everyone fidgets a tiny bit if they're nervous, like maybe smoothing out their skirt or adjusting their glasses, but I certainly don't sit around doing strange, repetitive things. I try very hard to sit still and be polite. I wouldn't want anyone to think I was anxious or... or acting peculiar. 

My mother always taught me to keep my hands folded nicely in my lap, and I try my best to do just that. I don't want to draw any negative attention to myself, you know? I hope that's the kind of answer you were looking for, Doctor. Am I being helpful?

Holding an... an uncomfortable position? Like a strange pose? 

Oh, my heavens, no. I don't think my old joints would even let me do that if I tried! I just sit normally, like I am right now. I always try to sit up straight and proper, just like I was taught. I would never twist myself up into some odd shape or hold an uncomfortable pose for no reason. 

I'd be so worried someone might see me and think I was making a scene or acting peculiar. I just try to be comfortable and keep my hands resting quietly. I really try my best to just be normal and not cause any fuss.

Do I feel uncomfortable or... afraid of being alone? Oh, no, not at all. 

I'm perfectly fine on my own. I have my little routines, you know? I keep the house tidy, and I read my Bible, and I listen to the radio. I don't mind the quiet one bit. I wouldn't want you to think I'm one of those needy people who always has to have someone fussing over them or holding their hand. 

And if, heaven forbid, something happened and a friendship ended... well, I'd just pray on it and keep busy. I certainly wouldn't go rushing out trying to find someone new just to fill the time. That wouldn't be very dignified, would it? I just try to be content with what the Lord gives me. 

I'm very independent, really I am. I hope that sounds alright? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right picture of how I live.
